    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Let me analyze what I see from the logs:

1. **reeval_flat_nli_s43** (cuda:1): Inference at 13% (8/62 batches), ~53-58s/batch. Started 7m49s ago. Model loaded correctly with SFT adapter. Looks normal.

2. **eval_plan_012_dpo_1ep_fixed_r2** (cuda:3): Inference at 56% (35/62 batches), ~112s/batch. Running for ~1h06m. This is the DPO 1ep re-eval WITH --sft_adapter. 

Wait, I'm noticing something odd here. The previous eval without the SFT adapter already showed rel_f1=0.0022, and the notes indicate DPO isn't suitable for this task at all. But now it's being re-evaluated with the adapter flag added—so this is testing whether the SFT adapter fixes the issue that caused the first run to fail.

Let me check the registry more carefully. The original eval_plan_012_dpo_ced_1ep command didn't include --sft_adapter, which means it might have had the same bug that affected the flat-NLI s43 evaluation. So that rel_f1=0.0022 result could be invalid, and the conclusion that DPO is completely unsuitable might have been premature. 现在我意识到之前的结论可能基于有缺陷的评估——同样的 bug（缺少 --sft_adapter）也出现在 eval_plan_001_flat_nli_s43 中，导致 rel_f1=0.0022 被标记为无效。所以那个没有 --sft_adapter 的 DPO 1ep 评估也很可能是无效的，真正的测试应该是修复后的 eval_plan_012_dpo_1ep_fixed_r2。

现在看几个正在运行的训练任务：plan_001_flat_nli_seed44 已经完成了 507/507 步（100%），目前在第 2.96 个 epoch，loss 约 0.57，entropy 约 0.61，mean_token_accuracy 约 0.857，看起来训练快要结束了，可能在做最后的 checkpoint 或评估。plan_001_flat_nli_seed45 则在进行某个评估阶段，进度 90-92%（903/985），应该是训练过程中的评估步骤。

现在检查 GPU 分配是否与实际命令中的 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 匹配。

所有四个实验都正确分配到了各自的 GPU（seed43 到 cuda:1，seed44 到 cuda:0，seed45 到 cuda:2，DPO 实验到 cuda:3），而且服务器有 4 个 RTX5090 GPU，没有看到特定的 gpu_ids 限制。关键发现是报告显示所有 GPU 空闲，但实际上所有 4 个都在运行实验，而且 DPO 结论可能过早，因为 plan_012_dpo 在评估时没有使用 --sft_adapter，这和 flat-NLI seed43 的 bug D064 一样。

现在正在运行修复版本的重新评估，结果可能会完全改变 DPO 的结论。

我需要仔细检查之前的 plan_012_dpo_ced 实验，看看它是否也存在同样的 --sft_adapter 缺失问题，因为那个实验显示 rel_f1=0.3429 但存在过拟合，这可能也是由于相同的配置错误导致的。

现在看到 eval_plan_012_dpo_1ep_fixed_r2 正在用 --sft_adapter 重新运行，这说明 Agent 确实发现了这个 bug，所以之前关于"D061 已关闭 DPO 实验线"的结论可能是基于有缺陷的评估得出的。

但 3ep DPO 的评估结果（rel_f1=0.3429）我看不到具体的 eval 命令，只能从审阅者的警告推断出存在严重过拟合的问题——3 个 epoch 导致奖励准确度达到 1.0，但边际值膨胀了 9 倍，熵也崩塌了。

现在的困惑是：3ep 版本的 0.3429 相比 1ep 的 0.0022，这个差异很奇怪，尤其是如果两者都没有用 --sft_adapter 的话。

但看起来 3ep 应该是用了适配器才对，因为没有适配器的话不会有这么高的分数。所以真实情况应该是 1ep 那次评估缺少了适配器，导致得出了错误的"DPO 完全不适用"的结论。Agent 识别出了这个 bug 并重新运行了评估，但注册表里还保留着那个过早的结论，这需要标记一个警告。 plan_012_dpo_ced_1ep: "DPO+CED 完全失败。1ep eval: rel_f1=0.0022 (34 TPs / 19265 preds)，比 3ep (0.3429) 更差。训练指标正常 (accuracy=0.82, margins=0.86)，但模型输出几乎无法匹配正确 relation。evi_f1=0.75 说明模型仍生成 evidence，但 relation extraction 崩溃。358/985 truncated (36%)。DPO 对 evidence-grounded DocRE 完全不适用——不是过拟合问题，是方法本身失效。D061 已关闭 DPO 实验线。"

不过这个结论是基于有问题的 eval（缺少 --sft_adapter），现在正在重新评估来验证。我需要检查 decisions.yaml 里是否真的有 D061 这个决策，同时也要看看 plan_011_bf16_g8_step_eval 的最新结果——这个刚完成的实验显示在不同训练步数上的性能指标，step_30/50/70 的 rel_f1 都在 0.43 左右，evi_f1 保持在 0.80 以上。

关键发现是所有中间检查点都低于最终的 ckpt-100（rel_f1=0.4549），说明 GRPO 训练在整个 100 步过程中持续改进，没有早停的收益。现在我需要聚焦最紧迫的问题：验证 DPO 的结论，然后检查 decisions.yaml 里的相关决策。
```

The DPO eval is running roughly twice as slow per batch as the flat NLI one—likely because the DPO model generates longer sequences, with about 36% of outputs getting truncated. I should post my findings about the DPO reliability issue once I have the corrected results.

I also need to verify whether the seed44 training actually finished, since the progress bar shows 100% completion but the process is still running. seed44 has finished training and is now in the checkpoint-saving phase. Rather than verify further, I'll compile my findings: the report shows all four GPUs are actively running experiments, the DPO conclusion may have been premature due to a buggy eval setup, the plan_011 results confirm ckpt-100 as the best GRPO checkpoint with non-monotonic performance across earlier checkpoints, and all running experiments appear healthy with reasonable GPU utilization across the board. [info] seed44 training at 100% (507/507 steps), seed45 in mid-training eval phase

The main actionable finding is #2 — the DPO conclusion. But since the re-eval is already running, the Agent has already caught and is fixing this. I should note this as a warning to not act on the "DPO failed" conclusion until the re-eval completes.

Actually, wait — let me re-read the registry entry for plan_012_dpo_ced_1ep more carefully:

"DPO+CED 完全失败。1ep eval: rel_f1=0.0022 (34 TPs / 19265 preds)...DPO 对 evidence-grounded DocRE 完全不适用——不是过拟合问题，是方法本身失效。D061 已关闭 DPO 实验线。"

This is in the registry notes. The Agent referenced D061 based on the buggy eval, but they've now recognized the issue and are running the corrected re-eval, so the registry conclusion and D061 decision both need to be revisited once that completes. I'm also noticing the DPO model's eval is running at roughly double the speed of the flat-NLI baseline — about 112s/batch versus 55s/batch — which suggests it's generating significantly longer outputs, and that length difference could be masking other issues with the approach.

Now I need to write up my findings clearly.

exp_id: mvp2_sft_eval_r2\\n    role: baseline\\n    result: F1=40.57%, Evi-F1=79.79%, EDCR=71.18%\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D011\\n- id: D014\\n  date: 2026-05-14 14:58 UTC\\n  summary: CED r5 mode collapse 确认 kill；CED reward 必须加 F1 门控防止短输出 hack；暂停 ced_kl001\\n  rationale: CED r5 在 65% 进度完全 collapse：completion 固定 34 tokens，f1_reward=0，ced_reward≈0.99，entropy≈0。模型发现极短输出不引用\\n    distractor 即可获得近完美 CED 分——CED 自身被 reward hack。根因：CED reward 不依赖抽取质量，模型可在 F1=0\\n    时仍获高 CED reward。修复方案：F1 门控——仅在 completion 包含 ≥1 个 predicted triple 时计算 CED reward，否则\\n    CED=0。这确保 evidence quality 的评估以有效抽取为前提。ced_kl001 使用同样有缺陷的 reward 设计，必须暂停，等修复后再启动。extract_r3\\n    部分 collapse 但仍有 F1 信号，继续观察。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  -
